useCallback, PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';\nimport { PhysicsCanvasProps, BallItem } from '../types';\n\nconst BALL_COLORS = [\n  '#38bdf8', // sky\n  '#818cf8', // indigo\n  '#c084fc', // purple\n  '#f472b6', // pink\n  '#fb7185', // rose\n  '#34d399', // emerald\n  '#fbbf24', // amber\n  '#60a5fa', // blue\n];\n\nconst getRandomColor = () => {\n  return BALL_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BALL_COLORS.length)];\n};\n\nexport default function PhysicsCanvas({\n  config,\n  balls: propBalls,\n  onBallCountChange,\n  onAddBall,\n}: PhysicsCanvasProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n\n  // 物理演算用ボールリスト（高頻度更新のためrefで管理）\n  const ballsRef = useRef<BallItem[]>(propBalls || []);\n  const configRef = useRef(config);\n  configRef.current = config;\n\n  // 次のボールID管理用\n  const nextIdRef = useRef<number>(1);\n\n  // ドラッグ/スリングショット状態\n  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);\n  const dragStartRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n  const dragCurrentRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n\n  // 親から渡されたballsの同期\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (propBalls !== undefined) {\n      ballsRef.current = propBalls;\n      if (propBalls.length > 0) {\n        const maxId = Math.max(...propBalls.map((b) => b.id), 0);\n        if (maxId >= nextIdRef.current) {\n          nextIdRef.current = maxId + 1;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }, [propBalls]);\n\n  // ボール追加用関数\n  const createBall = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, radius: number): BallItem => {\n      const ball: BallItem = {\n        id: nextIdRef.current++,\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx,\n        vy,\n        radius,\n        color: getRandomColor(),\n        mass: radius * radius, // 質量は面積に比例\n      };\n      return ball;\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // キャンバスのサイズ調整\n  const updateCanvasDimensions = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    canvas.width = Math.floor(rect.width * dpr);\n    canvas.height = Math.floor(rect.height * dpr);\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (ctx) {\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    updateCanvasDimensions();\n    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {\n      updateCanvasDimensions();\n    });\n\n    if (containerRef.current) {\n      resizeObserver.observe(containerRef.current);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', updateCanvasDimensions);\n    return () => {\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', updateCanvasDimensions);\n    };\n  }, [updateCanvasDimensions]);\n\n  // 物理演算ループ & レンダリングループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n\n    const step = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const width = rect.width;\n      const height = rect.height;\n\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      const isPaused = Boolean(currentConfig.isPaused);\n      const balls = ballsRef.current;\n\n      // 1. 物理シミュレーションの更新（一時停止でない場合）\n      if (!isPaused && width > 0 && height > 0) {\n        const { gravity, bounce, friction } = currentConfig;\n\n        // 位置の更新と重力・摩擦・壁バウンド\n        for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {\n          const b = balls[i];\n\n          b.vy += gravity;\n          b.vx *= friction;\n          b.vy *= friction;\n\n          b.x += b.vx;\n          b.y += b.vy;\n\n          // 左壁との衝突判定\n          if (b.x - b.radius < 0) {\n            b.x = b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * bounce;\n          }\n          // 右壁との衝突判定\n          else if (b.x + b.radius > width) {\n            b.x = width - b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * bounce;\n          }\n\n          // 天井との衝突判定\n          if (b.y - b.radius < 0) {\n            b.y = b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * bounce;\n          }\n          // 床との衝突判定\n          else if (b.y + b.radius > height) {\n            b.y = height - b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * bounce;\n\n            // 微小な上下動による床でのジッター防止\n            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2 && Math.abs(gravity) > 0) {\n              b.vy = 0;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ボール同士の衝突判定・応答\n        const ballsLen = balls.length;\n        for (let i = 0; i < ballsLen; i++) {\n          for (let j = i + 1; j < ballsLen; j++) {\n            const b1 = balls[i];\n            const b2 = balls[j];\n\n            const dx = b2.x - b1.x;\n            const dy = b2.y - b1.y;\n            const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const minDist = b1.radius + b2.radius;\n\n            if (distSq < minDist * minDist && distSq > 0) {\n              const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n              const nx = dx / dist;\n              const ny = dy / dist;\n\n              // 相対速度\n              const rvx = b2.vx - b1.vx;\n              const rvy = b2.vy - b1.vy;\n              const velAlongNormal = rvx * nx + rvy * ny;\n\n              // 近づいている場合のみ衝突応答を計算（離脱中は処理しない）\n              if (velAlongNormal < 0) {\n                // 反発係数は平均値を使用\n                const restitution = bounce;\n                const impulse =\n                  (-(1 + restitution) * velAlongNormal) /\n                  (1 / b1.mass + 1 / b2.mass);\n\n                const impulseX = impulse * nx;\n                const impulseY = impulse * ny;\n\n                b1.vx -= (1 / b1.mass) * impulseX;\n                b1.vy -= (1 / b1.mass) * impulseY;\n                b2.vx += (1 / b2.mass) * impulseX;\n                b2.vy += (1 / b2.mass) * impulseY;\n\n                // 重なり補正（位置補正）\n                const percent = 0.8; // 補正率\n                const slop = 0.01;\n                const penetration = minDist - dist;\n                if (penetration > slop) {\n                  const correction =\n                    (Math.max(penetration - slop, 0) /\n                      (1 / b1.mass + 1 / b2.mass)) *\n                    percent;\n                  const corrX = correction * nx;\n                  const corrY = correction * ny;\n\n                  b1.x -= (1 / b1.mass) * corrX;\n                  b1.y -= (1 / b1.mass) * corrY;\n                  b2.x += (1 / b2.mass) * corrX;\n                  b2.y += (1 / b2.mass) * corrY;\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // 2. 描画\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 背景グリッド描画（視覚的なガイドライン）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      const gridSize = 40;\n      for (let x = 0; x < width; x += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, height);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      for (let y = 0; y < height; y += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(0, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(width, y);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // ボールの描画\n      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {\n        const b = balls[i];\n\n        // ボール本体のグラデーション\n        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(\n          b.x - b.radius * 0.3,\n          b.y - b.radius * 0.3,\n          b.radius * data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/PhysicsCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useRef, useEffect, useState, useCallback, PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';\nimport { PhysicsCanvasProps, BallItem, SimConfig } from '../types';\n\nconst FALLBACK_CONFIG: SimConfig = {\n  gravity: 0.5,\n  bounce: 0.75,\n  friction: 0.99,\n  spawnRadius: 20,\n  isPaused: false,\n};\n\nconst BALL_COLORS = [\n  '#38bdf8', // sky\n  '#818cf8', // indigo\n  '#c084fc', // purple\n  '#f472b6', // pink\n  '#fb7185', // rose\n  '#34d399', // emerald\n  '#fbbf24', // amber\n  '#60a5fa', // blue\n];\n\nconst getRandomColor = () => {\n  return BALL_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BALL_COLORS.length)];\n};\n\nexport default function PhysicsCanvas({\n  config = FALLBACK_CONFIG,\n  balls: propBalls,\n  onBallCountChange,\n  onAddBall,\n}: PhysicsCanvasProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n\n  // 物理演算用ボールリスト（高頻度更新のためrefで管理）\n  const ballsRef = useRef<BallItem[]>(propBalls || []);\n\n  // configが未定義や不完全なプロパティでも安全にフォールバック\n  const safeConfig = {\n    ...FALLBACK_CONFIG,\n    ...(config || {}),\n  };\n  const configRef = useRef<SimConfig>(safeConfig);\n  configRef.current = safeConfig;\n\n  // 次のボールID管理用\n  const nextIdRef = useRef<number>(1);\n\n  // ドラッグ/スリングショット状態\n  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);\n  const dragStartRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n  const dragCurrentRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n\n  // 親から渡されたballsの同期\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (propBalls !== undefined) {\n      ballsRef.current = propBalls;\n      if (propBalls.length > 0) {\n        const maxId = Math.max(...propBalls.map((b) => b.id), 0);\n        if (maxId >= nextIdRef.current) {\n          nextIdRef.current = maxId + 1;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }, [propBalls]);\n\n  // ボール追加用関数\n  const createBall = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, radius: number): BallItem => {\n      const validRadius = typeof radius === 'number' && !isNaN(radius) && radius > 0 ? radius : 20;\n      const ball: BallItem = {\n        id: nextIdRef.current++,\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx,\n        vy,\n        radius: validRadius,\n        color: getRandomColor(),\n        mass: validRadius * validRadius, // 質量は断面積に比例\n      };\n      return ball;\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // キャンバスのサイズ調整\n  const updateCanvasDimensions = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    canvas.width = Math.floor(rect.width * dpr);\n    canvas.height = Math.floor(rect.height * dpr);\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (ctx) {\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    updateCanvasDimensions();\n    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {\n      updateCanvasDimensions();\n    });\n\n    if (containerRef.current) {\n      resizeObserver.observe(containerRef.current);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', updateCanvasDimensions);\n    return () => {\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', updateCanvasDimensions);\n    };\n  }, [updateCanvasDimensions]);\n\n  // 物理演算ループ & レンダリングループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n\n    const step = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const width = rect.width;\n      const height = rect.height;\n\n      // 未定義プロパティ安全ガード（gravity, bounce, friction, spawnRadius）\n      const currentConfig = {\n        ...FALLBACK_CONFIG,\n        ...(configRef.current || {}),\n      };\n\n      const isPaused = Boolean(currentConfig.isPaused);\n      const balls = ballsRef.current;\n\n      const gravity = typeof currentConfig.gravity === 'number' ? currentConfig.gravity : 0.5;\n      const bounce = typeof currentConfig.bounce === 'number' ? currentConfig.bounce : 0.75;\n      const friction = typeof currentConfig.friction === 'number' ? currentConfig.friction : 0.99;\n      const spawnRadius = typeof currentConfig.spawnRadius === 'number' ? currentConfig.spawnRadius : 20;\n\n      // 1. 物理シミュレーションの更新（一時停止でない場合）\n      if (!isPaused && width > 0 && height > 0) {\n        // 位置の更新と重力・摩擦・壁バウンド\n        for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {\n          const b = balls[i];\n\n          b.vy += gravity;\n          b.vx *= friction;\n          b.vy *= friction;\n\n          b.x += b.vx;\n          b.y += b.vy;\n\n          // 左壁との衝突判定\n          if (b.x - b.radius < 0) {\n            b.x = b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * bounce;\n          }\n          // 右壁との衝突判定\n          else if (b.x + b.radius > width) {\n            b.x = width - b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * bounce;\n          }\n\n          // 天井との衝突判定\n          if (b.y - b.radius < 0) {\n            b.y = b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * bounce;\n          }\n          // 床との衝突判定\n          else if (b.y + b.radius > height) {\n            b.y = height - b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * bounce;\n\n            // 微小な上下動による床でのジッター防止\n            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2 && Math.abs(gravity) > 0) {\n              b.vy = 0;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ボール同士の衝突判定・応答\n        const ballsLen = balls.length;\n        for (let i = 0; i < ballsLen; i++) {\n          for (let j = i + 1; j < ballsLen; j++) {\n            const b1 = balls[i];\n            const b2 = balls[j];\n\n            const dx = b2.x - b1.x;\n            const dy = b2.y - b1.y;\n            const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const minDist = b1.radius + b2.radius;\n\n            if (distSq < minDist * minDist && distSq > 0) {\n              const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n              const nx = dx / dist;\n              const ny = dy / dist;\n\n              // 相対速度\n              const rvx = b2.vx - b1.vx;\n              const rvy = b2.vy - b1.vy;\n              const velAlongNormal = rvx * nx + rvy * ny;\n\n              // 近づいている場合のみ衝突応答を計算（離脱中は処理しない）\n              if (velAlongNormal < 0) {\n                const restitution = bounce;\n                const m1 = b1.mass || 1;\n                const m2 = b2.mass || 1;\n                const impulse =\n                  (-(1 + restitution) * velAlongNormal) /\n                  (1 / m1 + 1 / m2);\n\n                const impulseX = impulse * nx;\n                const impulseY = impulse * ny;\n\n                b1.vx -= (1 / m1) * impulseX;\n                b1.vy -= (1 / m1) * impulseY;\n                b2.vx += (1 / m2) * impulseX;\n                b2.vy += (1 / m2) * impulseY;\n\n                // 重なり補正（位置補正）\n                const percent = 0.8;\n                const slop = 0.01;\n                const penetration = minDist - dist;\n                if (penetration > slop) {\n                  const correction =\n                    (Math.max(penetration - slop, 0) /\n                      (1 / m1 + 1 / m2)) *\n                    percent;\n                  const corrX = correction * nx;\n                  const corrY = correction * ny;\n\n                  b1.x -= (1 / m1) * corrX;\n                  b1.y -= (1 / m1) * corrY;\n                  b2.x += (1 / m2) * corrX;\n                  b2.y += (1 / m2) * corrY;\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // 2. 描画\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 背景グリッド描画（視覚的なガイドライン）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      const gridSize = 40;\n      for (let x = 0; x < width; x += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, height);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      for (let y = 0; y < height; y += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(0, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(width, y);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // ボールの描画\n      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {\n        const b = balls[i];\n\n        // ボール本体のグラデーション\n        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(\n          b.x - b.radius * 0.3,\n          b.y - b.radius * 0.3,\n          b.radius * 0.1,\n          b.x,\n          b.y,\n          b.radius\n        );\n        grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');\n        grad.addColorStop(0.3, b.color);\n        grad.addColorStop(1, '#000000');\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = grad;\n        ctx.shadowColor = b.color;\n        ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n        // ハイライトの輪郭\n        ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4)';\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // 3. スリングショット（発射準備）ガイドの描画\n      if (dragStartRef.current && dragCurrentRef.current) {\n        const start = dragStartRef.current;\n        const current = dragCurrentRef.current;\n\n        // 引っ張りライン\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(start.x, start.y);\n        ctx.lineTo(current.x, current.y);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.8)';\n        ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);\n        ctx.stroke();\n        ctx.setLineDash([]);\n\n        // 発射予測ベクトルライン（逆方向）\n        const vx = (start.x - current.x) * 0.15;\n        const vy = (start.y - current.y) * 0.15;\n        const launchTargetX = start.x + vx * 6;\n        const launchTargetY = start.y + vy * 6;\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(start.x, start.y);\n        ctx.lineTo(launchTargetX, launchTargetY);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(244, 114, 182, 0.8)';\n        ctx.lineWidth = 3;\n        ctx.stroke();\n\n        // 発射予定ボールのゴーストプレビュー\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(start.x, start.y, spawnRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)';\n        ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';\n        ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n    };\n\n    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // ポインターイベントによるボール生成・投擲\n  const getCanvasCoords = (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!containerRef.current) return { x: 0, y: 0 };\n    const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n    return {\n      x: e.clientX - rect.left,\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    const coords = getCanvasCoords(e);\n    dragStartRef.current = coords;\n    dragCurrentRef.current = coords;\n    setIsDragging(true);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDragging) return;\n    dragCurrentRef.current = getCanvasCoords(e);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDragging || !dragStartRef.current || !dragCurrentRef.current) {\n      setIsDragging(false);\n      dragStartRef.current = null;\n      dragCurrentRef.current = null;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    } catch {\n      // 既にキャプチャが外れている場合は無視\n    }\n\n    const start = dragStartRef.current;\n    const current = dragCurrentRef.current;\n    const dx = start.x - current.x;\n    const dy = start.y - current.y;\n\n    // クリック（移動距離が極小）かスリングショット（引っ張り）かを判定\n    let vx = 0;\n    let vy = 0;\n    const dragDistance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n    if (dragDistance > 6) {\n      // スリングショット: 引っ張った反対方向に初速を付与\n      const powerMultiplier = 0.18;\n      vx = dx * powerMultiplier;\n      vy = dy * powerMultiplier;\n    } else {\n      // クリックのみの場合はわずかなランダム横速度\n      vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4;\n      vy = Math.random() * 2;\n    }\n\n    const currentSpawnRadius =\n      typeof configRef.current?.spawnRadius === 'number'\n        ? configRef.current.spawnRadius\n        : FALLBACK_CONFIG.spawnRadius;\n\n    const newBall = createBall(start.x, start.y, vx, vy, currentSpawnRadius);\n\n    if (onAddBall) {\n      onAddBall(newBall);\n    } else {\n      ballsRef.current = [...ballsRef.current, newBall];\n      onBallCountChange?.(ballsRef.current.length);\n    }\n\n    setIsDragging(false);\n    dragStartRef.current = null;\n    dragCurrentRef.current = null;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerCancel = () => {\n    setIsDragging(false);\n    dragStartRef.current = null;\n    dragCurrentRef.current = null;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      ref={containerRef}\n      className={`canvas-area ${isDragging ? 'dragging' : ''}`}\n      onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n      onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n      onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n      onPointerCancel={handlePointerCancel}\n      role=\"application\"\n      aria-label=\"物理シミュレーションキャンバス。クリックまたはドラッグしてボールを発射できます。\"\n      tabIndex={0}\n    >\n      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className=\"physics-canvas\" />\n    </div>\n  );\n}","language":"tsx"}

useCallback, PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';\nimport { PhysicsCanvasProps, BallItem, SimConfig } from '../types';\n\nconst FALLBACK_CONFIG: SimConfig = {\n  gravity: 0.5,\n  bounce: // rose\n  '#34d399', // emerald\n  '#fbbf24', // amber\n  '#60a5fa', // blue\n];\n\nconst getRandomColor = () => {\n  return BALL_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BALL_COLORS.length)];\n};\n\nexport default function PhysicsCanvas({\n  config = FALLBACK_CONFIG,\n  balls: propBalls,\n  onBallCountChange,\n  onAddBall,\n}: PhysicsCanvasProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | configが未定義や不完全なプロパティでも安全にフォールバック\n  const safeConfig = {\n    ...FALLBACK_CONFIG,\n    ...(config || {}),\n  };\n  const configRef = useRef<SimConfig>(safeConfig);\n\n  // nextIdRef = useRef<number>(1);\n\n  // ドラッグ/スリングショット状態\n  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);\n  const dragStartRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n  const dragCurrentRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n\n  // 親から渡されたballsの同期\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (propBalls !== undefined) {\n      ballsRef.current = propBalls;\n      if === 'number' && !isNaN(radius) && radius > 0 ? radius : 20;\n      const ball: BallItem = {\n        id: nextIdRef.current++,\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx,\n        vy,\n        radius: validRadius,\n        color: getRandomColor(),\n        mass: validRadius * validRadius, // 質量は断面積に比例\n      };\n      return ball;\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // キャンバスのサイズ調整およびリサイズ時のボール境界補正\n  const updateCanvasDimensions = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const width = rect.width;\n    const height = rect.height;\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);\n    canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (ctx) {\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n    }\n\n    // リサイズ時にボールが画面外へ取り残されないよう境界内にクリップ\n    if (width > 0 && Math.max(b.radius, width - b.radius);\n          if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * 0.5;\n        } else if (b.x - b.radius < 0) {\n          b.x = b.radius;\n          if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * 0.5;\n        }\n\n        if (b.y + b.radius > height) {\n          b.y = Math.max(b.radius, height - b.radius);\n          if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * 0.5;\n        } else if (b.y - b.radius < 0) {\n ResizeObserver(() => {\n      updateCanvasDimensions();\n    });\n\n    if (containerRef.current) {\n      resizeObserver.observe(containerRef.current);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', updateCanvasDimensions);\n    return () => {\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', updateCanvasDimensions);\n    };\n  }, [updateCanvasDimensions]);\n\n  // 物理演算ループ & レンダリングループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n\n    const step = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const width = rect.width;\n      const height = rect.height;\n\n      // 未定義プロパティ安全ガード（gravity, bounce, friction, spawnRadius）\n      const currentConfig = {\n        ...FALLBACK_CONFIG,\n        ...(configRef.current || {}),\n      };\n\n      const isPaused = Boolean(currentConfig.isPaused);\n      const balls = ballsRef.current;\n\n      const gravity = typeof currentConfig.gravity === 'number' ? currentConfig.gravity : 0.5;\n      const bounce = typeof currentConfig.bounce === 'number' ? currentConfig.bounce : 0.75;\n      const friction = typeof currentConfig.friction === 'number' ? currentConfig.friction : 0.99;\n      const spawnRadius = typeof currentConfig.spawnRadius === 'number' ? currentConfig.spawnRadius : 20;\n\n      // 1. 物理シミュレーションの更新（一時停止でない場合）\n      if (!isPaused && width > 0 && height > 0) {\n        // 位置の更新と重力・摩擦・壁バウンド\n        for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {\n          const b = balls[i];\n\n          b.vy += gravity;\n          b.vx *= friction;\n          b.vy *= friction;\n\n          b.x += b.vx;\n          b.y += b.vy;\n\n          // 左壁との衝突判定\n          if (b.x - b.radius < 0) {\n            b.x = b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * bounce;\n          }\n          // 右壁との衝突判定\n          else if (b.x + b.radius > width) {\n            b.x = width - b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * bounce;\n          }\n\n          // 天井との衝突判定\n          if (b.y - b.radius < 0) {\n            b.y = b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * bounce;\n          }\n          // 床との衝突判定\n          else if (b.y + b.radius > height) {\n            b.y = height - b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * bounce;\n\n            // 微小な上下動による床でのジッター防止\n            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2 && Math.abs(gravity) > 0) {\n              b.vy = 0;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ボール同士の衝突判定・応答\n        const ballsLen = balls.length;\n        for (let i = 0; i < ballsLen; i++) {\n          for (let j = i + 1; j < ballsLen; j++) {\n            const b1 = balls[i];\n            const b2 = balls[j];\n\n            const dx = b2.x - b1.x;\n            const dy = b2.y - b1.y;\n            const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const minDist = b1.radius + b2.radius;\n\n            if (distSq < minDist * minDist && distSq > 0) {\n              const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n              const nx = dx / dist;\n              const ny = dy / dist;\n\n              // 相対速度\n              const rvx = b2.vx - b1.vx;\n              const rvy = b2.vy - b1.vy;\n              const velAlongNormal = rvx * nx + rvy * ny;\n\n              // 近づいている場合のみ衝突応答を計算（離脱中は処理しない）\n              if (velAlongNormal < 0) {\n                const restitution = bounce;\n                const m1 = b1.mass || 1;\n                const m2 = b2.mass || 1;\n                const impulse =\n                  (-(1 + restitution) * velAlongNormal) /\n                  (1 / m1 + 1 / m2);\n\n                const impulseX = impulse * nx;\n                const impulseY = impulse * ny;\n\n                b1.vx -= (1 / m1) * impulseX;\n                b1.vy -= (1 / m1) * impulseY;\n                b2.vx += (1 / m2) * impulseX;\n                b2.vy += (1 / m2) * impulseY;\n\n                // 重なり補正（位置補正）\n                const percent = 0.8;\n                const slop = 0.01;\n                const penetration = minDist - dist;\n                if (penetration > slop) {\n                  const correction =\n                    (Math.max(penetration - slop, 0) /\n                      (1 / m1 + 1 / m2)) *\n                    percent;\n                  const corrX = correction * nx;\n                  const corrY = correction * ny;\n\n                  b1.x -= (1 / m1) * corrX;\n                  b1.y -= (1 / m1) * corrY;\n                  b2.x += (1 / m2) * corrX;\n                  b2.y += (1 / m2) * corrY;\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // 2. 描画\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 背景グリッド描画（視覚的なガイドライン）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      const gridSize = 40;\n      for (let x = 0; x < width; x += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, height);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      for (let y = 0; y < height; y += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(0, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(width, y);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // ボールの描画\n      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {\n        const b = balls[i];\n\n        // ボール本体のグラデーション\n        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(\n          b.x - b.radius * 0.3,\n          b.y - b.radius * 0.3,\n          b.radius * 0.1,\n          b.x,\n          b.y,\n          b.radius\n        );\n        grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');\n        grad.addColorStop(0.3, b.color);\n        grad.addColorStop(1, '#000000');\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = grad;\n        ctx.shadowColor = b.color;\n        ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n        // ハイライトの輪郭\n        ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4)';\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // 3. スリングショット（発射準備）ガイドの描画\n      if (dragStartRef.current && dragCurrentRef.current) {\n        const start = dragStartRef.current;\n        const current = dragCurrentRef.current;\n\n        // 引っ張りライン\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(start.x, start.y);\n        ctx.lineTo(current.x, current.y);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.8)';\n        ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);\n        ctx.stroke();\n        ctx.setLineDash([]);\n\n        // 発射予測ベクトルライン（逆方向）\n        const vx = (start.x - current.x) * 0.15;\n        const vy = (start.y - current.y) * 0.15;\n        const launchTargetX = start.x + vx * 6;\n        const launchTargetY = start.y + vy * 6;\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(start.x, start.y);\n        ctx.lineTo(launchTargetX, launchTargetY);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)';\n        ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';\n        ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n    };\n\n    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(step);\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // ポインターイベントによるボール生成・投擲\n  const getCanvasCoords = (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!containerRef.current) return { x: 0, y: 0 };\n    const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n    return {\n      x: e.clientX - rect.left,\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDragging || !dragStartRef.current || !dragCurrentRef.current) {\n      setIsDragging(false);\n      dragStartRef.current = null;\n      dragCurrentRef.current = null;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    } catch {\n      // 既にキャプチャが外れている場合は無視\n    }\n\n    const start = dragStartRef.current;\n    const current = dragCurrentRef.current;\n    const dx = start.x - current.x;\n    const dy = start.y - current.y;\n\n    // クリック（移動距離が極小）かスリングショット（引っ張り）かを判定\n    let vx = 0;\n    let vy = 0;\n    const dragDistance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n    if (dragDistance > 6) {\n      // スリングショット: 引っ張った反対方向に初速を付与\n      const powerMultiplier = 0.18;\n      vx = dx * powerMultiplier;\n      vy = dy * powerMultiplier;\n    } else {\n      // クリックのみの場合はわずかなランダム横速度\n      vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4;\n      vy = Math.random() * 2;\n    }\n\n    const currentSpawnRadius =\n      typeof configRef.current?.spawnRadius === 'number'\n        ? configRef.current.spawnRadius\n        : FALLBACK_CONFIG.spawnRadius;\n\n    const newBall = createBall(start.x, start.y, vx, vy, currentSpawnRadius);\n\n    if (onAddBall) {\n      onAddBall(newBall);\n    } else {\n      ballsRef.current = [...ballsRef.current, newBall];\n      onBallCountChange?.(ballsRef.current.length);\n    }\n\n    setIsDragging(false);\n    dragStartRef.current = null;\n    dragCurrentRef.current = null;\n  };\n\n className={`canvas-area ${isDragging ? './components/Controls';\nimport PhysicsCanvas from './components/PhysicsCanvas';\n\nconst DEFAULT_CONFIG: SimConfig = {\n  gravity: 0.5,\n  bounce: 0.75,\n  friction: 0.99,\n  spawnRadius: 20,\n  isPaused: false,\n};\n\nconst BALL_COLORS = [\n  '#38bdf8', // sky\n  '#818cf8', // indigo\n  '#c084fc', // purple\n  '#f472b6', // pink\n  '#fb7185', // rose\n  '#34d399', // emerald\n  '#fbbf24', //
